<a id="s13-2" aria-hidden="true"></a>

## `13.2` The authoritative source registry

This is the discovery ledger behind the research: every central-bank page, peer-reviewed article, rating-agency note, fintech filing and vendor rate card retrieved live during the 2025–2026 source-expansion pass. 1,548 unique sources, grouped into 7 institutional categories.

In the corpus registry 216 of its 1,764 registry rows repeated a URL that was already listed. Each URL is one row here — it keeps its first registry number and names every other number and every section that cites it. Rows stay in their original registry order.

| Ref | Category | Sources | Domains | Cited |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **13.2.1** | <a href="#s13-2-1">Academic Journals &amp; Economic Research</a> | 301 | 87 | 7 |
| **13.2.2** | <a href="#s13-2-2">Multilateral Institutions &amp; Standard-Setters (AAOIFI, IFSB, IsDB, WB, BIS, IMF)</a> | 369 | 32 | 19 |
| **13.2.3** | <a href="#s13-2-3">Regulators &amp; Central Banks (BNM, SC, SAMA, CMA, CBUAE, DFSA, ADGM, CBB, OJK, SBP, SECP)</a> | 265 | 34 | 39 |
| **13.2.4** | <a href="#s13-2-4">Credit Rating Agencies &amp; Global Benchmarks (Fitch, S&amp;P, Moody's, DinarStandard, LSEG)</a> | 102 | 13 | 11 |
| **13.2.5** | <a href="#s13-2-5">Islamic Financial Institutions &amp; Fintech Primaries (Banks, Sukuk, SCF, P2P, Brokerage)</a> | 218 | 41 | 25 |
| **13.2.6** | <a href="#s13-2-6">Ecosystem Hubs, Accelerators &amp; Venture Capital (Hub71, DIFC Hive, BFB, SVC, Jada, HASAN)</a> | 229 | 25 | 13 |
| **13.2.7** | <a href="#s13-2-7">Frontier AI &amp; Technology Infrastructure (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, DeepSeek, Alibaba, etc.)</a> | 64 | 26 | 10 |
| | **All categories** | **1,548** | **258** | **124** |

:::note[Reading the registry]
Registry numbers are stable and linkable from anywhere on the site — `#r1234` resolves as long as that URL still has a row. `cited in §1.16` jumps from a source back into the section that uses it, and every external link opens in a new window.
:::

<a id="s13-2-1" aria-hidden="true"></a>
